[04:25:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15933s) And J5, consider and adopt a resolution to amend the salary schedule effective September 20th, 2026.

[04:25:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15935s) Good evening, city council.
[04:25:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15936s) So before you tonight, for your consideration is the adoption of a resolution to approve the successor agreement with the service employees International Union Local 521 or SCIU, as well as to amend the salary schedule to implement the wage adjustments contained in the agreement.
[04:25:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15937s) SCAU is a bargaining unit comprised of non-supervisory and non-sworn personnel across all city departments.
[04:25:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15938s) SCIU represents approximately 77 classifications with 150 budgeted full-time equivalent staff positions this fiscal year.
[04:25:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15939s) And approximately 129 temporary employees.
[04:25:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15940s) The existing agreement for full-time and part-time benefited staff expired on June 30th and the agreement for temporary employees inspired on October 22, 2011.
[04:25:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15941s) The city held a hearing to receive public input before initiating negotiations on April 14th, and Charles Sakai was appointed by the city council to serve as the city's chief negotiator.
[04:25:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15942s) Mr. Sakai and human resources staff participated in negotiations with SCIU from May through August, reaching tentative agreement in late August.
[04:25:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15943s) SCAU members ratified the agreement on or about September 8th.
[04:25:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15944s) As part of the negotiations process, SCIU requested to combine the temporary employee bargaining agreement into the regular unit, so both groups will now be governed by one agreement.
[04:25:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15945s) To summarize the key terms of the agreement, it is for a 3 year term ending June 30th, 2029, with a general salary increase of 4% in year one and 3% in year 2 and 3, along with some targeted market-based adjustments for water-related positions.
[04:25:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15946s) The agreement includes a minimum commitment of 500,000 across years 2 and 3 to implement the results of the citywide classification and compensation study that is currently underway, and it's expected to be completed by April 2027.
[04:25:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15947s) The city's contribution to employee health benefits would increase 2 to 4% each year, tied to the consumer price index, and there's the addition of one fixed holiday for Juneteenth with an equivalent reduction in floating holiday hours.
[04:25:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15948s) As I mentioned, we created a new section and updated various existing provisions to incorporate temporary employees into the agreement, which includes an adjustment to the paid time off accrual rate and beginning balance.
[04:25:49](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15949s) Starting in year two, the city will establish a certification recognition program to incentivize employees to obtain and maintain certificates of expertise in various disciplines above and beyond what is required for their position.
[04:25:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=15950s) Finally, there are modifications to existing callback pay, standby pay, bilingual pay.
[04:36:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16560s) commuter benefits, tuition, reimbursement, and rest periods, along with some cleanup related to nondiscrimination and military leave for civilian deployments.
[04:36:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16561s) The total fiscal impact for the proposed 3 year agreement is about 2800000 or an average cost of 929,000 per year.
[04:36:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16562s) Factoring in the wage and benefit placeholders that were incorporated into the current fiscal year budget.
[04:36:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16563s) Staff is requesting an additional $347,652 for the personnel budget and $108,324 in the operating budget.
[04:36:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16564s) The proposed salary schedule amendments will incorporate the wage adjustments for SCIU represented positions, as well as incorporate some formatting changes related to temporary and confidential employees to help add some clarity.

[04:37:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16628s) Here to introduce the item is Deputy City Clerk, Sarah Sandoval.
[04:37:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16629s) Good evening, Mayor Vice Mayor City Council, city, staff, and community members.
[04:37:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16630s) Let me go ahead and pull up our presentation.
[04:37:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16631s) Okay, my name is Sarah Sandoval, and I am your deputy city clerk here to present updates to advisory body work plans for the 2026 2027 work plan cycle.
[04:37:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16632s) For this presentation, we will do a brief overview of city council policy for work plans as well as highlight key changes made to advisory body work plans as proposed by the commissions who drafted them, after which we will ask the city council for their feedback.
[04:37:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16633s) Once feedback is provided, staff recommends that the city council approve the work plans with any provided edits included.
[04:37:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16634s) The work plan process is provided for in city council policy, CC dash 24-004, which is listed in the staff report as attachment G. Per the policy, each advisory body or commission is required to develop an annual work plan, which serves as a roadmap for commission activity for that following year.
[04:37:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16635s) Work plans must be approved annually by September 30th.
[04:37:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16636s) Please see here a list of Menlo Park commissions, each with their own focus area, each commission is a 7 member body comprised of resident volunteers who bring immense talent, care, and passion to the projects that they bring forward every year.
[04:37:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16637s) With the exception of the planning commission, the functions of which are governed by municipal code and state law, every commission creates an annual work plan for the city council to review.
[04:37:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16638s) With this review and subsequent workplan approval this evening comes the opportunity for city council to provide feedback and direction to commissions on their goals and planned projects.
[04:37:19](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16639s) Okay, now we will very briefly highlight some of the items proposed in the 2026, 27 work plans, which reflect notable changes, from the work plans that were approved last year.
[04:37:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16640s) So for the complete streets commission work plan, which continues to prioritize transportation related programs, safe routes, and community engagement.
[04:37:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16641s) We would like to highlight, um, The change to goal number 6, which defers a development review pilot program to 202728 due to concerns related to transportation division vacancies, which may limit staff's ability to bring these items to the CSC for review.
[04:37:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16642s) For the environmental quality commission, which advises the city council on matters involving climate change, environmental protection and sustainability.
[04:37:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16643s) There is one language update to last year's work plan, which is the addition of environmental justice to goal number four.
[04:37:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16644s) For the finance and audit commission work plan, which carries forward most of the same goals as the previous work plan.
[04:37:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16645s) We would like to highlight an updated goal number six, which states organize community budget forums to educate the public and gather input on financial matters, such as revenue generation and cost recovery strategies.
[04:37:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gO3j2c4uaII&t=16646s) For the Housing Commission proposed 2026, 27 work plan.
